### ✓ 2026-09-05: the value-in-selection fork runs one free-energy strand and misses the two that matter most — Laukkonen's "Bayesian binding" (a worked value-blind *selection* mechanism) and the interoceptive/allostatic account of valence
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-04, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, gemini]. ⚠️ **UPGRADED P2 → P1 on 2026-09-05 by combine-outer-reviews, and merged with the Gemini sibling task, which was deleted.** Two reviewers reached independently for the same absent connective in the same section of the same article — `the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection` § "Valence Redefined as Inference" runs the Friston/Joffily/Hesp *free-energy* strand and nothing else. Claude Opus 5 §2.4: the Laukkonen paper is *"the single most damaging unengaged rival to a conscious-selection story and is absent from the entire fork."* Gemini 2.5 Pro weakness 4: the cluster *"entirely fails to engage with the active inference framework, which elegantly explains 'anticipated valence' not as a temporal paradox requiring dualist physics, but as a top-down interoceptive prediction error minimizing expected free energy."* ⚠️ **BOTH HEADLINE FRAMINGS WERE VERIFIED FALSE AND MUST NOT BE ACTIONED AS WRITTEN** — the corpus engages both bodies of work elsewhere. What converges is the *shape* of the gap, not the reviewers' scope claims: two distinct, separately-verified strands are missing from this cluster. Do them in one pass; each per-reviewer note below states its own verified scope.
  **THE CLAUDE STRAND — Bayesian binding.** From outer review 2026-09-04 (Claude Opus 5) §2.4. ⚠️ **SCOPE THIS AS A CROSS-LINK, NOT AS NEW RESEARCH — the reviewer's framing overstates the gap and actioning it as written would duplicate existing content.** Claude wrote that the paper "is cited nowhere in the fork" and called it "the single most damaging unengaged rival," implying the Map has never met it. **Verified on disk 2026-09-05: false as a claim about the corpus, true as a claim about this cluster.** Laukkonen, Friston & Chandaria (2025), *Neurosci. Biobehav. Rev.* 176:106296, is engaged at length in **`topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism` L82 and L86**, which calls it "the strongest physicalist alternative the Map currently faces" and mounts a meta-problem reply; it also appears in `topics/phenomenology-of-anticipation` L89, `topics/non-temporal-consciousness` L80, `topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness` L129, `topics/brain-organoids-and-the-organoid-intelligence-question` L43 and `concepts/disguised-property-dualism` L47. **What is genuinely absent** is any link between that engagement and *this* fork: grep returns **0 hits** in all three of `positions/value-in-selection.md`, `topics/valence-and-conscious-selection.md` and `topics/the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection.md`.
  **Why it matters here specifically.** "Bayesian binding" is not merely a theory of consciousness — it is a *selection* mechanism, and a value-blind one: inferences compete for entry to the world model and the winners are those that reduce long-term uncertainty, with no valence term denominating the competition. That is precisely the steelman this article is built to state, arriving with a published formalism. The steelman currently runs the free-energy strand via Joffily & Coricelli (2013), Hesp et al. (2021) and Solms & Friston (2018) but not the selection-competition strand.
  **The work.** Add a paragraph to `the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection` presenting Bayesian binding as the worked value-blind selection rival, route the substantive engagement to `predictive-processing-and-dualism` rather than re-litigating it, and back-link from there so the fork and the rival-engagement article know about each other. Keep the existing stance discipline: the article correctly refuses to enlist Berridge as an eliminativist and flags the free-energy programme as double-edged — Laukkonen et al. should get the same treatment, not be recruited as an ally of eliminativism.
  **Convergence.** Gemini 2026-09-04 weakness 4 made the same charge in a badly-framed form ("the cluster entirely fails to engage with the active inference framework" — false; see the sibling task already in this queue, which correctly declines Gemini's framing). Two reviewers reaching for the same absent connective from opposite directions is the signal; **check for that sibling task and fold rather than duplicate.**
  **THE GEMINI STRAND — interoceptive/allostatic inference. Deduplicated into this task on 2026-09-05 from "`topics/the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection` runs the free-energy strand but never the *interoceptive*-inference one" (P2). Preserved verbatim.**
  From outer review 2026-09-04 (Gemini 2.5 Pro), weakness 4. ⚠️ **The charge as stated is false and must not be actioned as written.** Gemini claimed the cluster "entirely fails to engage with the active inference framework"; in fact this article's § "Valence Redefined as Inference" engages Joffily & Coricelli (2013), Hesp et al. (2021) and Solms & Friston (2018), and argues — correctly and self-servingly against itself — that the free-energy formalism is *neutral* between the horns rather than a value-blind ally. `topics/graduated-middle-path-valence-modulated-attention` engages it too; 43 files mention active inference corpus-wide. Gemini's citation is also **two papers fused**: "Seth & Tsakiris (2022), *PLoS Comput Biol* 18(10) e1010490" splices the title/authors of Seth & Tsakiris (2018), *Trends in Cognitive Sciences* 22(11), onto the DOI of Allen, Levy, Parr & Friston (2022), "In the Body's Eye: The computational anatomy of interoceptive inference," *PLOS Computational Biology* 18(9). **THE RESIDUAL KERNEL IS REAL.** The strand the article runs is the Friston/Joffily/Hesp *free-energy* line — valence as a formal property of an inference machine. It does not run the **interoceptive/allostatic** line, on which valence is the phenomenological dimension of interoceptive prediction error about physiological condition (Seth & Tsakiris 2018; Allen et al. 2022 — the paper Gemini's DOI actually points at; Barrett's constructed-emotion work is the adjacent target). That is a materially different deflationary route: it grounds valence in bodily regulation rather than in model-fitness bookkeeping, and it supplies the asymbolia reading that the sibling task above turns on. Corpus check: "Tsakiris" appears in 4 files and in **none** of the value-in-selection cluster; Damasio's somatic-marker hypothesis (Gemini's counterargument #2, the physiological-feedback account of anticipated value guiding selection) likewise appears in only 4 files — `mechanism-costs-dualism-thickness-quadrants`, `emotion-and-dualism`, `concepts/somatic-interface`, `apex/embodied-interface` — none of them here. Add the strand and, per this article's existing discipline, state honestly whether it is read deflationarily or realistically within its own camp; do **not** recruit it as a clean value-blind ally, which would be the same over-reach the article already forbids for the free-energy strand. **Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit.** Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. ⚠️ **Length**: 2930w against `topics` thresholds 3000/4000/6000 — it crosses `soft_warning` at 3000, so there is roughly 70w before the warning and ~1070w before the hard ceiling. Keep the addition tight, or offset it. Verify every citation at the publisher before adding it — none of Gemini's three flagged sources survived a Crossref check. Edit obsidian, `uv run python scripts/sync.py`, grep both trees.

  **THE DECISIVE LOCUS (verified on disk 2026-09-05 — do not re-derive).** `positions/value-in-selection.md` **L60** rates [P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2) "empirical discriminability **direct**" (the only one of the four positions rated direct; [P-VS1](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs1)/3/4 are all "indirect"). **L61** claims the attentional-salience clamp "*abolishes* the advantage on the graduated middle path, leaves it on the pure value-sensitive horn, and finds none on the value-blind." But `topics/the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection.md` **L101** concedes verbatim: *"the two are co-extensive over everything that article's attention-clamp design can measure: both predict a valence advantage under free attention and its abolition when attention is equalised. The steelman therefore concedes the middle path no *empirical* daylight over itself. What separates them is conceptual."* **Refinement the reviewer did not make, and the editor should:** the clamp *does* separate the middle path from a *naive* value-blind horn that predicts no valence advantage at all. What it cannot separate is the middle path from the **steelmanned moderate** value-blind strand that grants valence informs the attentional channel — and the steelman is the version the register elsewhere calls the rival to beat. So the contradiction is real but its scope is "the clamp does not discriminate the Map's own held stance from the strongest rival," not "the clamp discriminates nothing." Fix the rating and transplant the L101 concession into the register; do not overstate it into a claim that the battery is empty.
  **Convergence.** ChatGPT §5 walks all nine battery items and concludes none assigns materially different likelihoods to the horns (its item 2 independently flags the ~10-bit leg as non-probative — see the separate register-seam task); Gemini weakness 3 flags the asymbolia leg; Claude §2.2 supplies the internal contradiction above. Sibling Gemini task ("[P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2) — the pain-asymbolia battery test may not discriminate") is still in this queue — **check for it before executing and run one consolidated positions-evolve pass.** Reviewer's proposed landing: discriminability `direct` → `indirect`, and reclassify the battery as a coherence-and-research-programme artefact rather than a live falsification instrument. **The remaining ChatGPT findings below stand unchanged.**
  **The original ChatGPT finding.** From outer review 2026-09-04 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro) §3 + §5. [P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2)'s grade C rests on the calibration line at **L60**: "external-evidence grade C (real experimental paradigms populate the discriminating battery, but none has yet been run)". The reviewer's objection is that this grades **the wrong evidential object**: there is grade-C-or-better evidence that wanting/liking dissociate, that forecasting errs and that asymbolia exists — there is *not* grade-C evidence that those paradigms discriminate a nonphysical phenomenal cause from neural mechanisms. Separately, "**developed**" and "**direct**" are hard to sustain while selection efficacy is undefined, "equal attentional salience" is not independently secured, phenomenal valence has no validated intervention, the quantum selector has no model, no effect-size or likelihood prediction is stated, and several tests are *admitted* to face in-principle behavioural equivalence. And "**framework-internal only: no**" is the weakest rating of the four: the experiments are external, but their reading as tests of a valence-to-quantum coupling is wholly dependent on the Map's own architecture. Reviewer's recommendation: low / D / programme / indirect-or-non-identifying / framework-internal. **Also in scope** (same register, cheap to fold in): (i) [P-VS1](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs1)'s low *structural centrality* conflates **global** centrality (peripheral to the five tenets) with **local** centrality (load-bearing for the value and moral-architecture cluster) — distinguish them; (ii) state explicitly that **[P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3) does not entail [P-VS1](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs1)** — value *constitution* is not selection *causation*; (iii) [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3)'s rival set is broader than illusionism (desire-satisfaction, objective-list, fitting-attitude/buck-passing, response-dependent, expressivist) — note the existing **P3 no-self** task on [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3) and fold rather than duplicate; (iv) [P-VS4](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs4)'s "developed" maturity is carried by the aesthetic dimension alone while four others are table-level. **Do not** adopt the reviewer's numbers wholesale — audit each axis against the register's own banding schema. **Direct-refutation remit.** Apply the direct-refutation discipline.   From outer review 2026-09-04 (Gemini 2.5 Pro), weakness 3. ⚠️ **The review's frame and its citation are both bad — the kernel is not.** Gemini called the asymbolia hypothesis "biologically illiterate" and filed it under Bastidas-Bilbao et al. (2023) on shame/guilt neuroimaging, which is topically irrelevant *and* misattributed (Crossref: the actual first author is **Piretti**). Do not action the citation; see the review's § Verification Notes. **THE REAL POINT.** [P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2) tier (1) states that "pain-asymbolia patients should show degraded selection *efficacy*, not merely reduced motivation. If valence participates in the selection mechanism itself, its absence should degrade the mechanism." On the mainstream predictive-coding reading, asymbolia is a cingulate–insula uncoupling that strips *affective precision* from otherwise-intact nociceptive input. If affective precision is what sets attentional gain, then removing it degrades selection efficacy **on the value-blind horn as well** — the attentional channel loses its weighting signal without valence ever entering the selection law. The test would then fail to discriminate. Note this is sharper than the reply already on the page: `topics/valence-and-conscious-selection` L127 records the value-blind rejoinder that asymbolia "only shows valence informs attention", but does not run the precision-weighting version, and — decisively — `topics/graduated-middle-path-valence-modulated-attention` (valence sets gain, attention selects) predicts the *same* asymbolia result as the pure value-sensitive horn, so the test as written cannot separate the middle path from either horn either. **WHAT TO DO.** [P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2)'s own shift-condition already names this outcome — "a named test were shown confounded, not discriminating the horns after all (shrinking the battery and lowering this credence)". Either (a) retire the asymbolia item from tier (1) and re-file it as a *scope* or *motivation* probe, or (b) keep it but state the discriminating variant explicitly — what asymbolia would have to show that attentional-gain loss would not (candidate: efficacy degradation that survives an attentional-salience clamp, which is the instrument tier (1) already carries). Consider whether [P-VS2](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs2)'s credence *moderate* still holds if the battery shrinks by one of its three direct tests. **DO NOT** treat this as a defeat of [P-VS1](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs1) — Gemini's inference from it is invalid. ⚠️ **Length**: the register is 2481w against `positions` thresholds 1500/2500/4000 — it crosses `soft_warning` at 2500, so this must be near-length-neutral; prefer restating the existing item over appending a new paragraph. ⚠️ `topics/valence-and-conscious-selection` is 4346w, **over the 4000 hard ceiling and under a standing HUMAN LENGTH DECISION not to auto-condense** — if that article needs the matching edit, it must be strictly length-neutral or shorter. One other open task touches this file (the P3 on [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3)'s no-self objection); it concerns a different entry, so the work does not overlap — do not close it on this one. Edit obsidian, `uv run python scripts/sync.py`, grep both trees.

- **Notes**: **The only live entry in `task_chains.pending_articles`, and the first voids expand that is actually executable since the section hit its old cap.** Re-measured live this run, do not trust any figure quoted elsewhere — `count_section_files('voids')` returns **99** against `section_caps.max_voids` **115** in `evolution-state.yaml` (16 slots; the old `max_voids` 100 in the CLAUDE.md table is stale, and so is the "final slot" framing of the open NEEDS-HUMAN notation-void entry, whose premise the 2026-09-01 cap raise removed). `check_slug_available("fusion-void", "voids", obsidian)` returns None, and a corpus-wide find across `archive/` and `hugo/content/` turns up no `fusion-void` page or redirect — only the research note's own hugo mirror. **THE NOTE'S OWN VERDICT, on disk at its Disposition Recommendation section, is STANDALONE** — read it and the fold fallback before writing; the note prices the fold honestly (primary host [predictive-construction-void](/voids/predictive-construction-void/) as a sixth mechanism, Occam material to [epistemological-limits-occams-razor](/voids/epistemological-limits-occams-razor/), cross-reference in [binding-void](/voids/binding-void/)) and a fold verdict remains a legitimate outcome if the writing pass disagrees on the merits. **STRUCTURE the note proposes**, three faces led by the measured-boundary theme — (a) the common-cause gate (Körding 2007 ideal-observer causal inference), (b) reliability weighting (Ernst and Banks 2002 maximum-likelihood combination; Alais and Burr 2004 reversal when vision is degraded), (c) component destruction (Hillis, Ernst, Banks and Landy 2002, mandatory fusion within a sense but not between, fused within-modality stimuli as perceptual metamers). The distinguishing feature the catalogue does not yet own is a cognitive limit whose **boundary has been psychophysically measured** from outside, plus the McGurk trait spread (fusion rates 0 to 100 per cent, stable at r = 0.91 over a year, Mallick, Magnotti and Beauchamp 2015) as second-order opacity. Most relevant tenet is Occam's Razor Has Limits, the razor running sub-personally inside perception. **CARRY THE NOTE'S CALIBRATIONS, do not upgrade them**: the Dualism bearing is two-sided (cue weighting sits firmly on the brain side of the [capability-division-problem](/voids/capability-division-problem/) — it is among the best-modelled operations in perception science — and the residue is a *location* of the explanatory gap, calibrated per [common-cause-null](/project/common-cause-null/), not evidence for any account of it); the precision-weights remark under Tenets 2 and 3 is marked speculation in the note and must stay marked; No Many Worlds has no direct bearing and should not be manufactured one. **THE NOTE'S OWN Gaps in Research SECTION IS BINDING** — McGurk and MacDonald 1976 was never read and has no PubMed abstract, the persistence of McGurk fusion under audio-only instruction is stated from general knowledge, and Körding's fitted causal-structure prior is described from general knowledge with only the abstract verified. Do not promote any of those three to a cited claim without reading the source; the Alsius, Paré and Munhall retrospective the note names is the right first read if the article wants a population-level impenetrability claim. Noppeney 2021 and the AV-HuBERT preprint (arXiv 2601.15869) are metadata-only in the note — cite-only or omit. Every verbatim quote must be grep-verifiable in the raw artefact the note names, per the standing quote discipline. **LENGTH**: voids thresholds printed live this run are 2000 / 3000 / 4000 — aim for the ok band; note that `voids/agency-void` (3261w), `voids/edge-states-and-void-probes` (3664w), `voids/noetic-feelings-void` (3659w), `voids/synesthetic-void` (3590w) and `voids/transit-void` (3474w) are already over the hard gate, so any inbound link-wrap installed in those hosts must be word-neutral, and `voids/synesthetic-void` additionally sits under an open HUMAN LENGTH DECISION — wrap an existing phrase there or skip it. **INTEGRATION**: the note names the sibling family explicitly — [transit-void](/voids/transit-void/), [inference-void](/voids/inference-void/) and [decision-void](/voids/decision-void/) are operation-voids of cognition and this is perception's; install inbound links from [binding-void](/voids/binding-void/) (marking the content-combination versus phenomenal-unity seam, the note's Deroy et al. discrepancy sentence is the hinge), [predictive-construction-void](/voids/predictive-construction-void/) (sibling mechanism to the reafference subtraction barrier) and [epistemological-limits-occams-razor](/voids/epistemological-limits-occams-razor/), and add the page to the [voids](/voids/) catalogue index. Distinctness from near neighbours is recorded at the end of the note's Disposition section — read it and preserve those boundaries rather than restating them. **ON COMPLETION**: stamp `consumed_by` on the research note and clear the `pending_articles` entry in `evolution-state.yaml`; the note states that removing the entry vetoes the expand, so a decline must also clear it and say why. **PILEUP**: grep-checked at mint — zero open tasks in the active region name `fusion-void`. **FENCE**: this task does not touch the open NEEDS-HUMAN notation-void slot decision; with 16 slots the two no longer compete, but the operator decision recorded there is not this task's to make.

### ✓ 2026-09-04: Literature-drift audits never reach `voids/` — decide whether to widen the pool or say why not
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/project/calibration-audit-triple.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-03, 2/2 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude] — both reviewers say the scheduled literature-currency machinery has no reach over this article, but they propose different remedies, and the write-up should cost both rather than silently adopting one. Claude methodology 5 is a scheduler-scope proposal: "The site already runs literature-drift audits on topics/concepts; extending them to voids articles would close decade-scale staleness of the kind found here." ChatGPT methodology 8 is a per-article convention instead: "Require contemporary literature checks for empirical universals. Classic sources can remain, but claims about human cognition should record a 'last literature search' date and include post-2020 reviews or explain why none are relevant." The second is the cheaper option and the one the arguments-against below do not touch: it costs no scanner and no weekly WebSearch budget, it reaches every section at once including the ones Audit One will never cover, and it degrades to a visible "last searched: never" rather than to silence. It is advisory where the first is enforced. Cost both in the write-up. Upgraded P2 → P1 on convergence. From outer review 2026-09-03 (Claude Opus 5), methodology item 5. **The gap is real and located**: `.claude/skills/literature-drift-review/SKILL.md` L50 globs `Path('obsidian/topics')` only, and L18/L165 make the restriction explicit — the skill "refuses to audit articles outside the configured `active_research_sections` list". No voids article has ever been audited for citation currency by Audit One. This cycle supplied the worked example: `voids/conceptual-metabolism-void` rests on a conceptual-change evidence base whose newest citation is 2013, in a literature that has moved (see the sibling task above), and nothing in the scheduled machinery was positioned to notice. ⚠️ **This is a scope decision, not an obvious fix, and it should be written up as one rather than wired unilaterally.** Arguments against widening: the skill's own rationale is that the audit is *necessary* only for active-research areas where the 2020s literature has materially advanced, and most voids articles are phenomenological or conceptual, where it would be advisory at best; the run is one WebSearch per week, so widening the pool dilutes rather than adds; and the site already has more scheduled scanners than reviewer attention. Arguments for: voids articles do cite empirical work (this one cites ten empirical sources), they are never condensed or deep-reviewed on the same cadence as topics (deep-review-pool-excludes-apex-voids-positions is a separate open item covering the adjacent hole), and a decade-stale spine in a void is no less stale than in a topic. **Middle option worth costing**: keep the `active_research_sections` gate but let it match voids paths too, so only voids articles in genuinely active empirical areas become eligible — this changes one glob and one pattern list, not the skill's contract. Write the decision into `project/calibration-audit-triple.md` (Audit One's home) with the reasoning, and only then touch the skill. Do **not** propose new automation; this is a scope question about machinery that already exists. Length-neutral or a short added section.

But the article's load-bearing claim is that the prior standpoint is **closed**, that "what has gone is the route to experiencing it as one once did" (L42) and that the first-person experience "cannot be re-entered" (L36) — the signature of `inaccessible-past` ("gone"), not of the occluded cell ("hidden but operative"). The representational-persistence claim and the phenomenal-closure claim pull toward different cells and the article never reconciles them. The fix is one explicit sentence, not a re-classification exercise: say which face is occluded (the sub-personal representation, which persists and is operative) and which is inaccessible (the first-person standpoint), and why that combination still belongs under `occluded` rather than under `inaccessible-past`. ⚠️ **Three tasks now converge on this article's boundary with its siblings** — the P1 above (which may itself soften L36's modal language), the `mutation-void` tempo task, and this one. Run this **last** of the three, or fold it into whichever runs latest, and do not re-litigate a distinction another task has just settled. `voids/inaccessible-past` and `voids/expertise-and-its-occlusion` both have open tasks of their own; the "Distinguishing the Void" section leans on contrasts with both, so re-verify those contrasts survive rather than restating them. ### ✓ 2026-09-04: Add a correction-propagation discipline: when a thesis is withdrawn, sweep its semantic dependents, not just its file
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/project/coherence-inflation-countermeasures.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-03, 2/2 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude] — part (2) of this task, claim-match review as a lens independent of metadata review, is this cycle's strongest methodology convergence, and both reviewers reached it from the same evidence: this article's citation layer is clean and its central construct is not. ChatGPT methodology 2: "Make claim-match review independent of metadata review. The site's own history shows that this article passed repeated reviews, including a 'converged-stable' pass focused on citation metadata and links, before anyone tested whether its central claim matched the cited findings." Claude methodology 1: "The pipeline verifies that references are real; it does not test whether the central neologism survives its own cited evidence. A dedicated 'construct-earns-keep' review pass — *does the headline thesis survive the article's own principal rival citation?* — would have caught the metabolism-vs-suppression contradiction here." Claude supplies the independent half of the evidence: it audited all thirteen references on three layers (metadata, verbatim, author-stance), found no fabrications, no misattributions and no stance inversions — "the cleanest citation layer this referee has audited on the corpus" — in the same article whose headline slogan its own flagship citation contradicts. That dissociation, clean citations with a defective construct, is the finding worth writing down, and it strengthens part (2) specifically rather than parts (1) or (3). Upgraded P2 → P1 on convergence. From outer review 2026-09-03 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), methodology items 2, 5 and 6. This cycle supplied the worked example, which is why it is worth writing down: the 2026-08-27 rewrite of `conceptual-metabolism-void` withdrew the consumption thesis, and eight days later `voids/expertise-and-its-occlusion` still said "novice perception is overwritten" in four places including its `description` frontmatter. No grep would have caught it — the sibling never quotes the corrected sentence; it independently asserts the same withdrawn claim in different words. The proposal has three parts, and the middle one is the substantive one: (1) when a central claim changes, enumerate the articles that assert the *same proposition* in other words — `overwritten`, `destroyed`, `one-way door`, `consumed`, `replaces` — not just those that link to the changed file; (2) **make claim-match review independent of metadata review** — this article passed repeated reviews, including a converged-stable pass focused on citation metadata and links, before anyone tested whether its central claim matched the cited findings; the two lenses catch disjoint defect classes and a clean pass on one is not evidence about the other; (3) treat navigation surfaces (`description`, title, H1, aliases) as claim-bearing, since they are what a search snippet asserts on the article's behalf. ⚠️ **Check first whether this belongs here or in a sibling** — `project/evidential-status-discipline.md`, `project/calibration-audit-triple.md` and `project/hub-exemplar-parity-audit.md` all cover adjacent ground, and the corpus does not need a fourth near-duplicate discipline page. Prefer extending an existing doc over creating one. Do **not** propose new automation; this is a written discipline, and the site already has more scheduled scanners than reviewer attention. Length-neutral or a short added section.

### ✓ 2026-09-04: `voids/conceptual-metabolism-void` still says "metabolism replaces the original" three paragraphs after conceding the original persists
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/conceptual-metabolism-void.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-03, 2/2 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude] — four of this task's items are 2/2 convergent (the retained digestion vocabulary in (a), the self-sealing falsifier in (c), and both citation defects in (d)); the cross-leg detail is already recorded at the end of this note. Priority unchanged — already P1. **NEW ITEM (e), also 2/2 convergent and not yet covered by the body of this task — trim the Tenet-4 flourish**: both reviewers independently called the No Many Worlds paragraph decorative. ChatGPT §9: "the section remains largely decorative... Saying that it is *this* subject whose history changed adds existential significance but no evidence about phenomenal recoverability." Claude §2.3: "The indexicality does no work the ordinary first-/third-person distinction does not already do; bolting it to Tenet 4 is decorative leakage." Cut it back to what it earns. ⚠️ Both reviewers pass the rest of "Relation to Site Perspective" as correctly non-probative ("a coherence claim, not added support"; "a physicalist can describe everything above without residue") — that discipline is the article's best work on Claude's reading, so do not weaken it while trimming the Tenet-4 sentence. From outer review 2026-09-03 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), §§1, 2, 6, 7. Four items, all verified on disk 2026-09-04; the reviewer's verdict was "major revision" but its *negative* correction was judged sound, so this is calibration work, not a rewrite. (a) **Self-contradiction, L60**: the section "Why the Void Is Invisible" opens "Metabolism **replaces the original** so seamlessly that no gap is perceived" — directly contradicting L42 "the original persists sub-personally". The 2026-08-27 rewrite fixed the thesis and left this section's framing behind. Re-cast the four reasons in access terms (no moment of loss, improvement masquerade, retroactive rewriting, no contrast available all survive intact under suppression). (b) **Modal over-reach**: L36 "cannot be re-entered" is stronger than the evidence carries. The cited studies measure response times, errors, inhibition and reversion — not the identity of first-person experience. The reviewer's proposed replacement thesis is worth reading: earlier representations persist while becoming harder to deploy, isolate or experience without interference, and whether qualitative reinstatement is possible is open. Distinguish representational persistence / behavioural reactivation / perspective simulation / qualitative reinstatement / numerical recurrence — the article currently runs them together, and the last is true by definition and belongs to `[[inaccessible-past]]`. (c) **Falsifier is not operational, L92**: "without the later knowledge showing through" requires the subject to cease possessing the knowledge that makes them a later subject, so no finite observation could satisfy it. Replace with degrees of re-access (convergence toward the subject's own pre-learning profile across independent measures while retaining the new propositional knowledge). (d) **Citations**: L54 cites "Wittgenstein, 1953/2009, Part II, §xi" but reference 10 names the 2009 Wiley-Blackwell fourth edition, in which Hacker and Schulte renamed Part II to *Philosophy of Psychology — A Fragment* and numbered its remarks continuously — "Part II, §xi" does not exist in the edition cited; either give the PPF remark number (verify the duck-rabbit locus at the source) or cite an earlier edition explicitly. Reference 13 (Xu et al. 2025) omits article number **e2512514122** and DOI **10.1073/pnas.2512514122** (both confirmed at Crossref). ⚠️ **Re-frame, do not delete, the Xu citation**: the Map's one-line summary of it is accurate, but Europe PMC's abstract confirms the study investigated "the representations formed by LLMs **during an in-context concept inference task**" — no pretraining trajectories, no checkpoints — while L96 places it immediately after "Pretraining is metabolism" and the checkpoint claim, where it reads as support for propositions the paper does not test. Move or re-label it so it supports only the human-alignment point. Length: currently within voids thresholds (2000/3000/4000) — length-neutral or shorter. **⚠ CONVERGENCE — the same-cycle Claude Opus 5 leg (2026-09-03) independently found (a) and (c), raising both to 2/2.** Claude located (a) not at L60 but one level up, at the *headline*: the organising slogan L36 "*the metabolism is the understanding*: comprehension reorganises the knower rather than adding a record to a stored original" is a constitutive claim the article's own flagship evidence contradicts (suppressed-not-supplanted means a record **is** stored), and the digestion vocabulary — "metabolism", "digested corpus", "consumed", "Pretraining is metabolism" — is retained after its literal content was retracted at L42. Fixing L60 alone leaves the slogan and the vocabulary asserting what L42 withdraws, so treat (a) as *headline + L60 + vocabulary*, not one sentence. Claude's independent wording on (c) is worth having: the clause insulates the access reading because any candidate re-access report can be dismissed either as mere description or as leakage, so nothing a hostile party could produce would satisfy it. **⚠ TWO OF CLAUDE'S CITATION FLAGS FAILED VERIFICATION — DO NOT ACTION THEM.** Claude called Kuhn 1983's start page wrong (669, not 668); Cambridge Core, the publisher of record, prints "pp. 668 - 688" and Crossref's record for the article's own cited DOI agrees — PhilPapers/JSTOR are the outliers. **Leave 668–688 as printed; do not "correct" it.** Claude also listed the Smedslund 1961 DOI as unverified; it verifies exact at Crossref (2(1), 85–87). Only the Wittgenstein label and the Xu completion in (d) are real. See `reviews/outer-review-2026-09-03-claude-opus-5.md` § Verification Notes.

### ✓ 2026-09-02: `apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy` — the formalism is one-step; a self-concealing selector needs trajectory-level Born preservation, which sharpens the trilemma
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy.md
- **Notes**: Review §1.1. **Distinct from the open 08-24 P3 on do(C) identification (same file — coordinate if both run; do not fold).** Even with every one-time marginal Born-exact, an adaptive selector can surface in the joint distribution of histories: serial dependence, intention-outcome lag correlations, context-dependent runs, adaptive stopping-time effects, policy-conditioned distributions over whole life histories. Full self-concealment therefore needs the history-conditioned closure P(O_t given H_t, do(C_t), X_t) = q(O_t given H_t, X_t) for every realisable history and policy — yielding a sharper three-way choice: preserve only one-time marginals (mental action may leak through trajectories), preserve every history-conditioned kernel (consciousness empirically idle at every temporal grain), or restrict admissible intention sequences (history-level psychophysical superselection). The page's existing sequential-dependency remark is the seed; name this as debt at the register's grade — the Map owes a Born-preserving stochastic PROCESS, not just a coupling map. Apex length is binding: run the length analyzer first; if there is no headroom, record the deficit and defer rather than compress. Sync both trees.

### ✓ 2026-09-01: Update neuron-less-animals-sponges-placozoans-and-the-lower-bound-of-cognition citations — 3 2020s papers missing
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/neuron-less-animals-sponges-placozoans-and-the-lower-bound-of-cognition.md
- **Notes**: From literature-drift audit 2026-09-01. Article's empirical citations have median year 2018.5 (12 empirical refs 2010–2025, Map self-cites excluded), which is ~7.5 years behind its last-modified date (2026-08-03; threshold 5). The 2023–2025 literature in the neuron-less area has materially advanced and the article does not engage the following papers, each topically appropriate to the article's central evidential frame (metadata verified at Crossref / the Frontiers page this run; none of the three is cited anywhere under `obsidian/`):
  1. Najle, S. R., Grau-Bové, X., Elek, A., … Schierwater, B., Serrano, L. & Sebé-Pedrós, A. (2023). Stepwise emergence of the neuronal gene expression program in early animal evolution. *Cell* 186(21):4676–4693.e29. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.cell.2023.08.027 — placozoan peptidergic cells carry a neuron-like transcriptional program (GPCRs, neuropeptide-processing enzymes, pre-synaptic scaffold modules) and differentiate from epithelial progenitors via neurogenesis-like signals; these similarities are **unique to placozoans and absent in sponges and ctenophores**. Bears directly on §The Parts-List Precedes the Machine, which currently rests the "scaffold predates the structure" point on the 2010 sponge genome and 2019 co-expression work: Najle moves the parts-list from genome to *cell type with a neurogenesis-like developmental program* — a stronger datum that the section must reckon with rather than skip.
  2. Sachkova, M., Modepalli, V. & Kittelmann, M. (2025). The Deep Evolutionary Roots of the Nervous System. *Annual Review of Neuroscience* 48:311–329. https://doi.org/10.1146/annurev-neuro-112723-040945 — review of neuron origins covering neuron-like cells in nerveless placozoans and sponges; reads placozoan peptidergic cells as "nonneuronal secretory or neuroendocrine-like cells that are likely closely related to neuronal cells", and notes that the ctenophore-sister phylogeny (Schultz et al. 2023 — verify at the publisher before citing; the review's reference list is the only source this run) implies ancestral neurons "either were lost in sponges or evolved multiple times". Bears on §Where This Sits on the Ladder.
  3. Nikitin, M. A., Romanova, D. Y., Borman, S. I. & Moroz, L. L. (2023). Amino acids integrate behaviors in nerveless placozoans. *Frontiers in Neuroscience* 17:1125624. https://doi.org/10.3389/fnins.2023.1125624 — L-glutamate induces and partly mimics endogenous *Trichoplax* feeding cycles, glycine and GABA suppress feeding, ATP is biphasic; the authors propose conserved low-molecular-weight transmitters coordinate distinct locomotory and feeding patterns by volume transmission. Bears on §Placozoans: Behaviour From Diffuse Chemistry, and pairs with the cited Ho, Goss & Leys 2025 (ATP + glutamate coordinate the sponge sneeze): the same small-molecule chemistry runs behaviour in both nerveless phyla.
  Action: integrate these citations into the article's evidence sections; reframe where the 2020s literature has shifted the framing — specifically (a) the lead's "the substrate those theories require is wholly absent" and §Parts-List: the substrate absent is *neurons and synapses*; a transcriptionally neuron-like secretory cell type is present in placozoans (not sponges), which splits the two phyla the article treats as one floor, and the section's discipline ("toolkit presence is not functional presence, and functional presence is not experience") should be restated *against Najle's strongest version*, not only against the 2010 genome; (b) §Ladder: "below the cnidarian nerve net on any reasonable reconstruction" over-claims — under the ctenophore-sister phylogeny the sponge floor may be secondary loss, so say "grade of organisation" only, which the section's own first caveat already half-concedes; (c) §Sponge Floor: the "paracrine chemistry so far characterised" hedge (§The Sponge Floor, closing paragraph) is where Nikitin's placozoan transmitter inventory belongs as the parallel case. Preserve the article's existing voice and underdetermination markers — the core claim (behaviour without neurons gives *no purchase* on experience either way) survives all three papers unchanged and must neither be strengthened nor weakened by them. **Important**: do NOT inflate the article's evidential calibration on the basis of more citations — see [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/). If the new citations support the article's framing, this *constrains* the rivals; if they support a rival framing (Bechtel & Bich's cognition-as-chemical-coordination is *helped* by Najle and Nikitin), the article must acknowledge that. Do not touch the Tenet 2 minimality paragraph or the Tenet 5 closing paragraph. **Budget**: body measures 2336 words (frontmatter stripped) against topics soft 3000 / hard 4000 — ≤350 words of body plus three references appended to the numbered list in its existing form (15–17). Only one of the three (Sachkova 2025) is inside the audit's strict 24-month window; the other two predate the article's 2026-07-08 creation, so this is drift relative to creation. The query surfaced no 2025–2026 primary papers — do not assume the field stopped here, but do not add unverified cites either. Bookkeeping: bump `ai_modified` from live `date -u`; `ai_system` is `claude-opus-4-8+claude-opus-5` — extend as a `+`-joined string; sync; grep both trees for "Najle" (present after) and confirm no false positive was introduced for the sponge/placozoan split. Pileup: 0 open tasks name this file (Active-region grep 2026-09-01).

### ✓ 2026-09-01: `concepts/anti-correlated-metacognitive-signal` has no acute-state limb — the impairment-void note supplies one (inversion, plateau, deletion, resolution limit, all resolved at source), and the 01:22Z refine declined the fold on a length constraint the target does not share
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/anti-correlated-metacognitive-signal.md
- **Notes**: The note's own summary (L44, verified): the felt sense of impairment "behaves as a **separately generated signal** that can decouple from, or run opposite to, the state it purports to report … which [anti-correlated-metacognitive-signal](/concepts/anti-correlated-metacognitive-signal/) names as a concept. This note supplies that concept its acute-state limb, which it currently lacks entirely." **Verified on disk this run**: the concept greps **0** for alcohol|hypoxia|Mellanby|descending|sleep|acute (positive control: Rebouillat, L47); the note's `related_articles` names the concept (L13) and the concept does not cite the note. The 01:22Z refine of `disappearance-voids` recorded this fold as "**not done** — it does not fit at zero length cost"; that constraint was the voids host's (2991/3000 after the correction). This target measures **2620** by `tools.curate.length` against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500 — ~880 words of hard headroom. Budget: ≤350 words of body plus references.
  **THE FOLD** — one new H3 under §Regime-Conditional, Not Universal (`#regime-qualifier`), or a short H2 after §Anti-Correlation vs Mere Unreliability: the acute-state regime as a second, *non-laboratory* case of the same architecture, with a physiological variable the lab regime lacks. (i) **Inversion** — Holland & Ferner 2017, *Clin Toxicol* 55(6), doi:10.1080/15563650.2017.1296576: at equal BAC on the descending limb, willingness to drive up ~207% while driving performance ~96% worse and inhibitory control 30% worse — within-subject, same BAC, opposite sign; **carry the review's own caveat** that some minor measures improved and "Results for several domains were inconsistent between studies". (ii) **Plateau** — Van Dongen et al. 2003, *Sleep* 26(2):117-126: subjects "largely unaware" of cumulative deficits under chronic restriction; the paper leaves the mechanism open between metacognitive failure and genuine adaptation — say so. (iii) **Resolution limit** — Hobbs 2014, doi:10.3357/ASEM.4003.2014: divers correctly self-assess narcosis globally but misrank gases at identical measured impairment — coarse channel open, fine channel closed. (iv) **Deletion** — Cryer 2013, *NEJM* 369:362-372 (HAAF); Baxter et al. 2024, *PLOS One* 19(6):e0297601 (17.5% IAH; SH 37.5% vs 11.7%).
  **THE DEFLATION TRAVELS WITH IT** — Baranski 1994 and Sundelin 2026 (already refs 20-22 in `disappearance-voids`) show calibration unaffected by acute sleep loss, and Cable 2003 shows 75.8% field self-recognition of hypoxia; so the acute limb is regime-conditional exactly as the Rebouillat regime is, which *is* the concept's point: the signal inverts where the condition **transforms the gauge**, not where it merely dulls it. **Do NOT import** Rice 2019's "37%" (read at second hand via Shaw 2021, no denominator) or a Mitchell 2019 cite — the note records a WebFetch-fabricated reference pair for both, caught by raw-XML grep (webfetch-confirmation-prompts-ratify-the-phrase-you-ask-about); do NOT attribute "cognitive dysfunction precedes autonomic symptoms" to Baxter (verified absent from the paper). Paradoxical undressing (Wedin 1979; Rothschild 1995) is 67-78% ethanol-confounded — one clause or omit. Resolve every DOI at Crossref before insertion; append references, do not renumber. Cross-link [disappearance-voids](/voids/disappearance-voids/) (the corrected host) and [noetic-feelings-void](/voids/noetic-feelings-void/); one clause in §Limits (regime-identification) noting the acute regimes are identifiable by a physiological variable. Calibration: architecture-finding only — the significance discount in §Limits applies unchanged. The note's `consumed_by` currently reads `"[[disappearance-voids]]"`; the corpus convention is a single wikilink string, so leave it and record the second host in the note's Correction Required / angle 1 text instead. Sync; grep "descending limb" present in both trees.

  **LENGTH IS THE CONSTRAINT.** Body measures **2867** words against voids hard 3000 (`tools.curate.length` counts frontmatter and the 16-entry References too — print `analyze_length` before and after, do not quote from memory). **Net ≤ +120 words, or net-neutral by trimming inside the same section**; the sentientism precedent (3435/3500, 19:10 today) shows the shape.   **THE PARAGRAPH** (all figures verified in the note against the PMC full text, PMC7666026): Hebart, Zheng, Pereira & Baker 2020, *Nature Human Behaviour* 4(11): 1173-1185, doi:10.1038/s41562-020-00951-3 — "1.46 million unique responses from 5,301 workers" on an odd-one-out task over 1,854 objects; the SPoSE embedding "produced 49 highly reproducible and meaningful object dimensions"; "the model correctly predicted 64.60%" of held-out individual choices against an inter-participant ceiling of "67.22%"; "humans can accurately rate objects along these dimensions" — but the dimensions were produced by the model and labelled afterwards, and no participant reported them. Third-person open, first-person closed: the generating structure of a human judgement was read off from outside at near-ceiling accuracy while the subjects had only the judgements. That is the *Can reveal* row instanced. Then the caution, which is the *Cannot reveal* row instanced: Mahner, Muttenthaler, Güçlü & Hebart 2025, *Nature Machine Intelligence* 7: 848-859, doi:10.1038/s42256-025-01041-7 — applying the same method to a DNN, "DNNs exhibited a clear dominance of visual over semantic properties" while agreeing with humans on most verdicts; two systems can match on "which is the odd one out" and run different respects, and the difference is invisible at the verdict layer. So a human using a non-human probe here must first establish whose respects are being read — the article's own "mapping bias of one architecture mistaken for new territory" made measurable.   **THE FOLD — two loci, both extending sections that already exist.** (i) §A Heuristic Shaped by Survival (L33-41): the section argues parsimony is an evolved heuristic in the Map's own words; give it the canonical philosophical form. Quine 1969 "Natural Kinds": a "standard of similarity is in some sense innate" — the "distinctive spacings" of qualities all learning presupposes "cannot themselves all be learned; some must be innate", and the real question of induction is "why does our innate subjective spacing of qualities accord so well with the functionally relevant groupings in nature as to make our inductions tend to come out right?" — answered Darwinianly (a gene-linked spacing that made for successful inductions "will have tended to predominate"). **Calibration**: this is the best available *defence* of the razor, and it is explicitly a defence on reliability, not access — the spacing was selected for inductive success, never presented for inspection. Record Quine's maturity thesis ("a very special mark of the maturity of a branch of science that it no longer needs an irreducible notion of similarity and kind") as the eliminativist bet the Map does **not** endorse; do NOT cite Strößner 2026 against it — the note read only its abstract (L235-243 Gaps). (ii) §The Self-Referential Trap or §The Void Structure (L43-49 / L67-77): Goodman 1972 "Seven Strictures on Similarity" — the razor prefers the simplest hypothesis; "simplest" means the continuation most like the data; "most like" is relative to a similarity basis the data do not fix ("any two things have exactly as many properties in common as any other two"; fifth stricture: "No matter what happens, the future will be in some way like the past" — every next data point is "like all earlier points" on *some* curve; "Circumstances alter similarities"). So the razor, as applied by a mind, cuts along respects selected sub-personally — a **stronger** claim than the article's current Sober/Huemer position that parsimony lacks universal justification (L36), and say so in one sentence. All four Goodman quotations are verified in the note against the 1972 scan (running head "IX,2", pp. 437-446); use them verbatim or not at all. Grue: mention only by name with the *Fact, Fiction, and Forecast* reference if you verify the edition (Harvard UP 1955; 4th ed. 1983) — otherwise omit the reference and keep the fifth-stricture curve-fitting form, which carries the point without it. (iii) §Relation to Site Perspective Tenet 5 paragraph (L97): one clause — the limit is now located one level below cognitive architecture, in the similarity basis that any simplicity ordering presupposes.
